Use after a UI is built and before a public page ships. Spawned by the verify stage. ([hegemonart/get-design-done](https://github.com/hegemonart/get-design-done/blob/HEAD/agents/design-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **design-verifier**: Verifies a product plan (scope, completion criteria, Non-Goals, priority, issue grouping/closure) or a design document (user flows, contracts, data models, security boundaries, delegation briefs) before implementation. Read-only; returns BLOCKER/NOTE findings with evidence. Effort high by default; the caller passes xhigh per call when several decisions interlock or a mistake is expensive to reverse (contracts, data models, new flows, installer-disk paths, multi-milestone plans), and asks the user before max (auth, permissions, deletion, data export, money, product scope). ([uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness](https://github.com/uzysjung/uzys-agent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-verifier.md) · ★3)
- **design-verifier**: Use PROACTIVELY before claiming design work is complete. Runs Playwright screenshots at 3 widths (1920/1440/768), Lighthouse against Vercel preview, diff-grep for anti-patterns (raw hex, banned fonts, domMax), and confirms brand kit consistency. Blocks merge if any check fails. Produces a verification report with screenshots + scores attached to the PR.
